PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object b, object d craves object c, harmony, planet object b, planet object c, province object a and province object d.
My goal is to have that object c craves object a.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.

    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.

    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?y) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x)
                 (not (province ?x))
                 (not (planet ?y))
                 (not (harmony))
           )
  )
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x)
                 (planet ?x)
                 (harmony)
                 (not (pain ?x))
           )
  )
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?other - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony)
                 (province ?x)
                 (craves ?x ?other)
                 (not (province ?other))
                 (not (pain ?x))
           )
  )
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?other - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?other) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x)
                 (province ?other)
                 (not (craves ?x ?other))
                 (not (province ?x))
                 (not (harmony))
           )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem specific_instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects 
    object_a object_b object_c object_d - object
  )
  (:init 
    (craves object_a object_b)
    (craves object_d object_c)
    (harmony)
    (planet object_b)
    (planet object_c)
    (province object_a)
    (province object_d)
  )
  (:goal 
    (craves object_c object_a)
  )
)
</problem>
```

                 (not (province ?x))
                 (not (planet ?y))
                 (not (harmony))

    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x)
                 (planet ?x)

                 (not (pain ?x))

    :effect (and (harmony)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here's the sequence of actions to successfully achieve the final goal using the given PDDL plan:

1. **Action**: Feast  
   **Objects Involved**: Object D and Object C  
   **Description**: Object D, which craves Object C, and is currently located in a province under a state of harmony, moves to satisfy its craving. As a result, Object D experiences pain, Object C’s province status is shifted, and harmony is disrupted.

2. **Action**: Succumb  
   **Objects Involved**: Object D and Object A  
   **Description**: Now that Object D is in pain, it succumbs to Object A. This results in Object A being reinstated or established as a province and a planet, and harmony is restored. Additionally, Object D is no longer in pain.

3. **Action**: Attack  
   **Objects Involved**: Object C  
   **Description**: Object C, still in its self-contained province state (as it is a planet), targets an attack against itself. As a result, it falls victim to pain, loses its province and planet statuses, and harmony is disrupted again.

4. **Action**: Overcome  
   **Objects Involved**: Object C and Object A  
   **Description**: Finally, with Object C in a state of pain, it decides to overcome this by establishing new cravings targeted towards Object A. This action returns harmony to the environment, sets Object C as a province, and resolves its pain.

In the end, the goal is satisfied with Object C successfully craving Object A, completing all specified actions precisely without missing or adding any extra steps. The entire plan executes with a unit cost of 4.

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain transformation_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates 
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (object_craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )
  
  (:action Attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action Succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action Overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (object_craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action Feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (object_craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (object_craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan and domain information, here are the natural language instructions to achieve the goal:

1. **Feast Object b from Object c**:  
   Move object b into a desired state by feeding it from object c. This will cause object b to experience pain. Object c is no longer craved by object b.

2. **Succumb Object b**:  
   Revert object b to a peaceful state, ensuring it once again represents a province and a planet without experiencing pain.

3. **Feast Object c from Object d**:  
   Transition object c into a painful state by extracting resources from object d. As a result, object d will no longer be craved by object c.

4. **Succumb Object c**:  
   Return object c to a state of harmony and recover its status as a province and a planet, free from pain.

5. **Feast Object d from Object a**:  
   Induce a painful transformation in object d by consuming from object a. Object a will cease to be craved by object d during this action.

6. **Succumb Object d**:  
   Alleviate object d's pain, restoring its capacity as a province and planet with harmony.

7. **Attack Object a**:  
   Inflict pain on object a by targeting it directly, which results in the loss of its province, planet status, and harmony attributes.

8. **Overcome Object a from Object c**:  
   Establish a new craving relationship where object a desires object c, while object c's previous provincial status is revoked.

9. **Attack Object d**:  
   Redirect focus to object d, causing immediate pain and stripping away its provincial and planetary identifications.

10. **Overcome Object d from Object a**:  
    Develop a craving relationship where object d desires object a. This state change reinstates harmony for object d but not object a’s previous province status.

Through these sequential transformations, the desired final goal state is established: object a craves object c and object d craves object a.

I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

My goal is to have that object a craves object d and object d craves object b.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```
// Domain definition for the problem
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (not (pain ?x)) (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)) (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x) (province ?y))
  )
)
</domain>

// Problem definition for the problem
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects a b c d - object)
  (:init
    (craves a c)
    (craves c b)
    (craves d a)
    (harmony)
    (planet b)
    (province d)
  )
  (:goal (and
    (craves a d)
    (craves d b)
  ))
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is a sequence of human-readable actions following the provided plan to solve the problem:

1. **Feast object d from object a**: Object d starts craving object a, disrupting harmony, and causing object d to gain progress, while object a loses its province status.

2. **Succumb object d**: Object d regains harmony and its province and planetary status, resolving previous disruptions.

3. **Feast object a from object c**: Object a begins to crave object c passionately, leading to a craving swap that results in disharmony and causes object c to gain, with object a losing its status.

4. **Overcome object a from object d**: In this move, object a passionately overcomes object d, establishing harmony anew and renewing its craving for object d, which now persists.

5. **Feast object c from object b**: Object c initiates a craving for object b, producing disharmony and causing a swap. The results are object c's advancement at the expense of harmony and its province status.

6. **Succumb object c**: Demands from the previous feast diminish, restoring object c's harmony and reinstating its province and planetary status.

7. **Feast object a from object d**: Object a establishes a fervent craving for object d, resulting in a craving exchange that disrupts harmony, while object d remains craved.

8. **Succumb object a**: Object a's cravings are settled, ensuring harmony is reinstated, and object a regains its province and planetary attributes.

9. **Attack object d**: An aggressive step by object d results in provocation, removing its province and planetary status and disturbing harmony.

10. **Overcome object d from object b**: Object d handles distress by persistently achieving harmony once more, craving object b, through overcoming undesirable cravings.

11. **Attack object a**: Object a resorts to aggressive behavior, leading to disharmony, while forfeiting both its province and its planetary status.

12. **Overcome object a from object d**: Object a triumphs over its previous cravings, crafting harmony anew and establishing a consistent craving for object d.

Each action here is synchronized with the tasks and effects specified in the domain, resulting in the achievement of the final goal to have object a craving object d and object d craving object b.

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?subject - object ?object - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (not (pain ?x)) (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)) (province ?x) (harmony) (craves ?x ?y))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects object_a object_b object_c object_d - object)
  (:init
    (craves object_b object_a)
    (craves object_c object_b)
    (harmony)
    (planet object_a)
    (planet object_d)
    (province object_c)
    (province object_d)
  )
  (:goal (and
    (craves object_b object_a)
    (craves object_d object_c)
  ))
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
The task involves a sequence of actions performed on a set of objects, following specific rules and preconditions, to reach a final goal state. Here is the human-readable version of the plan that satisfies the problem using the given PDDL specification, domain, and problem. This sequence of actions provides step-by-step instructions on what actions to take:

1. **First Action: Attack Object D**
   - Execute the "attack" action on `object_d`.
   - Preconditions: 
     - `object_d` must be a province.
     - `object_d` must be a planet.
     - Harmony must be present.
   - Effects of this action:
     - `object_d` experiences pain.
     - `object_d` is no longer a province.
     - `object_d` is no longer considered a planet.
     - Harmony is lost.

2. **Second Action: Overcome Object C with Object D**
   - Execute the "overcome" action using `object_c` with `object_d`.
   - Preconditions:
     - `object_c` must be a province.
     - `object_d` must be experiencing pain.
   - Effects of this action:
     - `object_c` is no longer a province.
     - `object_d` no longer experiences pain.
     - `object_d` becomes a province.
     - Harmony is restored.
     - `object_d` now craves `object_c`.

Each action aligns with specific rules and preconditions set in the problem statement to achieve the final goal of having `object_b` crave `object_a` and `object_d` crave `object_c`. The sequence provided does not specify timestamps or explicit durations as the plan does not include temporal information; however, it maintains the sequence of actions necessary for reaching the goal state.

I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```lisp
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object_type)
  (:predicates 
    (province ?obj - object_type)
    (planet ?obj - object_type)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?obj - object_type)
    (craves ?obj1 ?obj2 - object_type))
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?obj - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj)
                 (not (province ?obj))
                 (not (planet ?obj))
                 (not (harmony))))
                 
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?obj - object_type)
    :precondition (pain ?obj)
    :effect (and (province ?obj)
                 (planet ?obj)
                 (harmony)
                 (not (pain ?obj))))
                 
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?obj ?other_obj - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?other_obj) (pain ?obj))
    :effect (and (harmony)
                 (province ?obj)
                 (craves ?obj ?other_obj)
                 (not (province ?other_obj))
                 (not (pain ?obj))))
                 
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?obj1 ?obj2 - object_type)
    :precondition (and (craves ?obj1 ?obj2) (province ?obj1) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj1)
                 (province ?obj2)
                 (not (craves ?obj1 ?obj2))
                 (not (province ?obj1))
                 (not (harmony))))
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    object_a object_b object_c object_d - object_type)
  (:init
    (craves object_a object_c)
    (craves object_c object_b)
    (craves object_d object_a)
    (harmony)
    (planet object_b)
    (province object_d))
  (:goal (and
    (craves object_a object_b)
    (craves object_d object_c)))
)
</problem>
```

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object_type)
  (:predicates 
    (province ?obj - object_type)
    (planet ?obj - object_type)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?obj - object_type)
    (craves ?obj1 ?obj2 - object_type))
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?obj - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?obj) (planet ?obj) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj)
                 (not (province ?obj))
                 (not (planet ?obj))
                 (not (harmony))))
                 
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?obj - object_type)
    :precondition (pain ?obj)
    :effect (and (province ?obj)
                 (planet ?obj)
                 (harmony)
                 (not (pain ?obj))))
                 
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?obj ?other_obj - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?other_obj) (pain ?obj))
    :effect (and (harmony)
                 (province ?obj)
                 (craves ?obj ?other_obj)
                 (not (province ?other_obj))
                 (not (pain ?obj))))
                 
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?obj1 ?obj2 - object_type)
    :precondition (and (craves ?obj1 ?obj2) (province ?obj1) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?obj1)
                 (province ?obj2)
                 (not (craves ?obj1 ?obj2))
                 (not (province ?obj1))
                 (not (harmony))))
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of actions that should be taken to accomplish the given task:

1. **Feast on Object A by Object D**: This action is performed because Object D craves Object A while being in a province and harmony state. As a result, Object D will switch its support to Object A.

2. **Succumb Object D**: Re-establishes the original conditions for Object D, granting it province, planet, and harmony status again. Meanwhile, the previous pain inflicted on Object D is nullified.

3. **Feast on Object C by Object A**: Object A, in a province and harmony state, feasts on Object C, satisfying its craving. Consequently, Object A experiences pain and transfers some of its properties to Object C.

4. **Succumb Object A**: Similar to Object D, Object A recovers its province status and reaches a harmonious state, nullifying the pain it previously experienced.

5. **Feast on Object B by Object C**: Object C, now in possession of province and harmony, consumes Object B, which it craves. This action causes Object C to become pained.

6. **Succumb Object C**: Object C reclaims its province status and recovers its harmony state, effectively nullifying any remaining pain.

7. **Attack Object A**: Initiated under conditions where Object A possesses province and planet attributes alongside harmony. This, however, results in pain, while the aforementioned attributes are invalidated.

8. **Overcome on Object B by Object A**: This action is performed when Object B is in a province state and Object A is in pain. As a result, Object A takes province status, harmony is restored, and Object A now craves Object B.

9. **Attack Object D**: Performed under conditions where Object D holds province and planet statuses along with harmony. This causes Object D to experience pain and lose its previous attributes.

10. **Overcome on Object C by Object D**: This final action is executed when Object C is in province and Object D is in pain. Object D gains province status, harmony is secured, and Object D shifts its craving to Object C.

Each action is taken systematically according to the plan, ensuring all actions are compliant with the given specifications. The final goal of having Object A crave Object B and Object D crave Object C is achieved.

FINAL-PLAN

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?o - object)
    (planet ?o - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?o - object)
    (craves ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?o - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?o) (not (province ?o)) (not (planet ?o)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?o - object)
    :precondition (pain ?o)
    :effect (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony) (not (pain ?o)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?o2) (pain ?o1))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?o1) (craves ?o1 ?o2)
              (not (province ?o2)) (not (pain ?o1)))
  )
  
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?o1 ?o2) (province ?o1) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?o1) (province ?o2) 
              (not (craves ?o1 ?o2)) (not (province ?o1)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    a b c d - object
  )
  (:init
    (craves a d)
    (craves b c)
    (craves d b)
    (harmony)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
  )
  (:goal
    (craves a c)
  )
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Let's follow the PDDL plan provided and translate each step into detailed human-readable actions:

1. **Feast Object `a` on Object `d`:**  
   Object `a`, located in its province and with the world in harmony, feasts on object `d`, which craves `a`.  
   *Effect:* Object `a` now feels pain and loses its province status and the harmony is broken. Object `d` loses its craving status from `a`.

2. **Succumb Action on Object `a`:**  
   Object `a`, feeling pain, succumbs.  
   *Effect:* Object `a` regains its province status and harmony is restored. The pain for object `a` is alleviated.

3. **Feast Object `d` on Object `b`:**  
   Object `d`, with the province and harmony restored, feasts on object `b`, which craves `d`.  
   *Effect:* Object `d` now feels pain and loses its province status and the harmony is broken. Object `b` loses its craving status from `d`.

4. **Succumb Action on Object `d`:**  
   Object `d`, feeling pain, succumbs.  
   *Effect:* Object `d` regains its province status and harmony is restored. The pain for object `d` is alleviated.

5. **Feast Object `b` on Object `c`:**  
   Object `b`, now in its province and with harmony, feasts on object `c`, which craves `b`.  
   *Effect:* Object `b` now feels pain and loses its province status and the harmony is broken. Object `c` loses its craving status from `b`.

6. **Succumb Action on Object `b`:**  
   Object `b`, feeling pain, succumbs.  
   *Effect:* Object `b` regains its province status and harmony is restored. The pain for object `b` is alleviated.

7. **Attack Action on Object `a`:**  
   Object `a`, in its province and with harmony established, attacks.  
   *Effect:* Object `a` now feels pain and loses its province status, as well as breaks the harmony.

8. **Overcome Object `a` with Object `c`:**  
   Object `a`, experiencing pain, overcomes with the help of object `c` that was its target.  
   *Effect:* Object `a` regains harmony and establishes a craving relationship with object `c`, achieving the desired goal. Object `a` also regains province status as part of its recovery process. 

*Result:* The sequence of actions successfully results in object `a` craving object `c`, fulfilling the final goal. Each step corresponds closely to the specified PDDL plan.

I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (object_craves ?x ?y - object)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (not (pain ?x)) (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)) (harmony) (province ?x) (object_craves ?x ?y))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (object_craves ?y ?x) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (object_craves ?y ?x)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x) (province ?y))
  )
)
</domain>
```

```pddl
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects a b c d - object)

  (:init
    (object_craves b c)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet c)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
    (province b)
    (province d)
  )

  (:goal (and
    (object_craves a b)
    (object_craves b d)
    (object_craves d c)
  ))
)
</problem>
```

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (object_craves ?x ?y - object)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (not (pain ?x)) (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)) (harmony) (province ?x) (object_craves ?x ?y))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (object_craves ?y ?x) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (object_craves ?y ?x)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x) (province ?y))
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects a b c d - object)

  (:init
    (object_craves b c)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet c)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
    (province b)
    (province d)
  )

  (:goal (and
    (object_craves a b)
    (object_craves b d)
    (object_craves d c)
  ))
)

   - Effect: Object 'b' experiences pain, and 'b' now possesses the characteristics of 'c'.

2. **Succumb Action**  
   - Have object 'b' recover from its pain.  
   - Precondition: Object 'b' is experiencing pain.  
   - Effect: Object 'b' becomes a province and planet again, and harmony is restored.

   - Attack object 'a'.  
   - Precondition: Object 'a' is a province and a planet under conditions of harmony.  
   - Effect: Object 'a' loses its province and planet attributes, and the state of harmony is disrupted; 'a' feels pain.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object c craves object d, object d craves object a, harmony, planet object a, planet object b, province object b and province object c.
My goal is to have that object a craves object c, object b craves object a and object d craves object b.

My plan is as follows:

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```lisp
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object_type)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object_type)
    (planet ?x - object_type)
    (pain ?x - object_type)
    (harmony)
    (craves ?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object_type)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    object_a object_b object_c object_d - object_type
  )
  (:init
    (craves object_c object_d)
    (craves object_d object_a)
    (harmony)
    (planet object_a)
    (planet object_b)
    (province object_b)
    (province object_c)
  )
  (:goal (and
    (craves object_a object_c)
    (craves object_b object_a)
    (craves object_d object_b)
  ))
)
</problem>
```

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object_type)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object_type)
    (planet ?x - object_type)
    (pain ?x - object_type)
    (harmony)
    (craves ?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object_type)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object_type ?y - object_type)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here are the actions you need to perform to achieve the final goal, following the given plan precisely:

1. **Feast (object_c from object_d):** Object `c` pursues its desire for object `d` while they are in a state of harmony and located in a province. As a result, object `c` experiences pain, and object `d` assumes the status of a province.

2. **Succumb (object_c):** Object `c` recovers from the pain, achieving a harmonious state and reassuming its province and planetary roles.

3. **Feast (object_d from object_a):** Object `d` fulfills its craving for object `a`, which involves both being in harmony and province status. This action causes object `d` to experience pain, and object `a` becomes a province.

4. **Overcome (object_d from object_b):** Object `d`, in pain and located within object `b`'s province, resolves its situation by reestablishing harmony and craving object `b`. Object `d` also adopts the province role, while object `b` is relieved of province status.

5. **Attack (object_a):** Object `a`, located in its province and planet, decides to attack, relinquishing its provincial role and harmony status, and undergoing pain.

6. **Overcome (object_a from object_c):** Object `a`, suffering from pain, resolves the situation by desiring object `c`, regaining harmony, and assuming the province status.

7. **Feast (object_d from object_b):** Object `d` fulfills its urge for object `b` under the conditions of harmony and provincial status. This results in pain for object `d`, while object `b` attains province status.

8. **Succumb (object_d):** Object `d` emerges from its pain, regaining harmony, and reinstating its province and planetary roles.

9. **Attack (object_b):** Object `b`, in its province and planetary state, attacks, relinquishing its province and harmony status, and experiencing pain.

10. **Overcome (object_b from object_a):** Object `b`, in pain within object `a`'s province, overcomes the situation by redirecting its craving to object `a`, reclaiming harmony, and adopting the province, while object `a` cedes its province status.

11. **Attack (object_d):** Object `d`, now prepared within its province and planet, launches an attack, resulting in pain and losing its provincial and harmonious state.

12. **Overcome (object_d from object_b):** In pain and within object `b`'s territory, object `d` overcomes by shifting its desire to object `b`, regains harmony, adopts the province role, and object `b` is relieved of its province status.

Each step should be carried out precisely and sequentially according to the plan to satisfy the final goal.

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates 
    (province ?o - object)
    (planet ?o - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?o - object)
    (craves ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?o - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?o) (not (province ?o)) (not (planet ?o)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?o - object)
    :precondition (pain ?o)
    :effect (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony) (not (pain ?o)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?o - object ?other - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?o))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?o) (craves ?o ?other) (not (province ?other)) (not (pain ?o)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?o - object ?other - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?o ?other) (province ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?o) (province ?other) (not (craves ?o ?other)) (not (province ?o)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2
------------------------------
Here is a detailed sequence of steps to achieve the goal of the problem using the given plan. Each action corresponds to a task performed with specific conditions and effects:

1. **Feast Action with Object C and Object B:**
   - **Task:** Object C feasts on Object B.
   - **Preconditions:** Object C craves Object B, Object C is in its province, and harmony exists.
   - **Effects:** Object C is in pain, Object B becomes part of a province, Object C no longer craves Object B, Object C loses its province status, and harmony is disrupted.

2. **Succumb Action on Object C:**
   - **Task:** Object C succumbs to its pain.
   - **Preconditions:** Object C is in pain.
   - **Effects:** Object C regains its province and planet status, harmony is restored, and Object C is no longer in pain.
   
3. **Attack Action on Object B:**
   - **Task:** Object B is attacked.
   - **Preconditions:** Object B is in its province, on its planet, and harmony exists.
   - **Effects:** Object B is in pain, loses its province and planet statuses, and harmony is disrupted.

4. **Overcome Action with Object B and Object C:**
   - **Task:** Object B overcomes its situation with Object C.
   - **Preconditions:** Object C is in its province, Object B is in pain, and Object B does not have a province.
   - **Effects:** Harmony is restored, Object B gains a province status, Object B craves Object C, Object C loses its province status, and Object B is no longer in pain.

5. **Feast Action with Object D and Object A:**
   - **Task:** Object D feasts on Object A.
   - **Preconditions:** Object D craves Object A, Object D is in its province, and harmony exists.
   - **Effects:** Object D is in pain, Object A becomes part of a province, Object D no longer craves Object A, Object D loses its province status, and harmony is disrupted.

6. **Succumb Action on Object D:**
   - **Task:** Object D succumbs to its pain.
   - **Preconditions:** Object D is in pain.
   - **Effects:** Object D regains its province and planet status, harmony is restored, and Object D is no longer in pain.

7. **Attack Action on Object A:**
   - **Task:** Object A is attacked.
   - **Preconditions:** Object A is in its province, on its planet, and harmony exists.
   - **Effects:** Object A is in pain, loses its province and planet statuses, and harmony is disrupted.

8. **Overcome Action with Object A and Object D:**
   - **Task:** Object A overcomes its situation with Object D.
   - **Preconditions:** Object D is in its province, Object A is in pain, and Object A does not have a province.
   - **Effects:** Harmony is restored, Object A gains a province status, Object A craves Object D, Object D loses its province status, and Object A is no longer in pain.

This sequence of actions ensures that Object A craves Object D and Object B craves Object C, achieving the specified problem goal.

FINAL-PLAN

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (object_craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?y) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?y)) (not (harmony)))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?y) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?y))
    :effect (and (province ?x) (object_craves ?x ?y) (harmony) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?y)))
  )
  
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (object_craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (object_craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>
```

```pddl
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    object_a object_b object_c object_d - object
  )
  (:init
    (object_craves object_a object_d)
    (object_craves object_b object_c)
    (harmony)
    (planet object_c)
    (planet object_d)
    (province object_a)
    (province object_b)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      (object_craves object_b object_d)
      (object_craves object_d object_a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_5
------------------------------
Here is a sequence of human-readable actions based on the provided PDDL plan:

1. **Step 1:** Start by having object A "feast" on object D.  
   - Action: Feast from D to A while there is harmony.
   - Result: Object A experiences "pain," object D becomes a new "province," and harmony is disrupted.

2. **Step 2:** Next, object D "overcomes" object A.  
   - Action: Overcome object A by object D which is in "pain."
   - Result: Object D craves object A, harmony is restored, object D becomes a new "province," and object A is no longer a "province."

3. **Step 3:** Then, object D "feasts" on object A.  
   - Action: Feast from A to D while there is harmony.
   - Result: Object D experiences "pain," object A becomes a new "province," and harmony is disrupted.

4. **Step 4:** After that, object B "overcomes" object D.  
   - Action: Overcome object D by object B, which is in "pain."
   - Result: Object B craves object D, harmony is restored, object B becomes a new "province," and object D is no longer a "province."

5. **Step 5:** Object B then "feasts" on object C.  
   - Action: Feast from C to B while there is harmony.
   - Result: Object B experiences "pain," object C becomes a new "province," and harmony is disrupted.

6. **Step 6:** Object A then "overcomes" object B.  
   - Action: Overcome object B by object A, which is in "pain."
   - Result: Object A craves object B, harmony is restored, object A becomes a new "province," and object B is no longer a "province."

7. **Step 7:** Object A now "feasts" on object B.  
   - Action: Feast from B to A while there is harmony.
   - Result: Object A experiences "pain," object B becomes a new "province," and harmony is disrupted.

8. **Final Step:** Finally, object D "overcomes" object A.  
   - Action: Overcome object A by object D, which is in "pain."
   - Result: Object D craves object A, achieving the final goal state.

Each action follows the plan precisely, utilizing explicit transitions from the PDDL plan, capturing the intricacies of the problem-solving sequence.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>
```

```pddl
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  
  (:objects
    a b c d - object
  )
  
  (:init
    (craves b d)
    (harmony)
    (planet a) (planet c) (planet d)
    (province a) (province b) (province c)
  )
  
  (:goal
    (and
      (craves b c)
      (craves d a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
```
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)

(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)

    a b c d - object
  )
  
  (:init
    (craves b d)
    (harmony)
    (planet a) (planet c) (planet d)
    (province a) (province b) (province c)
  )
  
  (:goal
    (and
      (craves b c)
      (craves d a)
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
To achieve the specified goals in the problem involving different objects and actions, follow the steps below in sequence. Each step corresponds to an action from the plan provided, aiming to transform the initial conditions into the desired goal state.

1. **Step 1: Feast Action**
   - Action: Feast `b` from `d`.
   - Description: Object `b` satisfies its craving for object `d`, thereby creating a state of pain for `b`. The harmony is disrupted, and object `d` regains a province status.
   - Effects: Pain is induced on `b`. Province status shifts to `d` from `b`, and harmony is lost.

2. **Step 2: Overcome Action**
   - Action: Overcome `b` with `c`.
   - Description: Object `b` regains harmony by satisfying its craving with object `c`. The effect is the establishment of craving satisfaction (`b` now craves `c`), while `b` shifts its province status towards `c`.
   - Effects: Harmony is restored. Object `b` holds a province after this action, and `b` craves `c`.

3. **Step 3: Attack Action**
   - Action: Attack `d`.
   - Description: Perform an attack action that causes `d` to experience pain, thereby losing its province and planet statuses alongside disrupting harmony.
   - Effects: Object `d` receives a state of pain and loses both province and planet statuses. Harmony is disrupted as a result.

4. **Step 4: Overcome Action**
   - Action: Overcome `d` with `a`.
   - Description: Object `d` manages to overcome pain and restore itself by craving `a`. This action re-establishes `d`'s harmony and province status, now craving object `a`.
   - Effects: Object `d` gains harmony and province status, fulfilling its craving for `a`, and no longer remains in pain.

By executing these actions sequentially, the goal state is achieved where `b` craves `c` and `d` craves `a`. The plan effectively utilizes the defined actions to transition from the initial conditions to the desired state without deviation.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

```lisp
<domain>
(define (domain object_manipulation)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
  )
  
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
  
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

    a b c d - object
  )

    (craves d a)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet b)
    (province c)
    (province d)
  )

    (craves a b)
  )
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having object 'a' crave object 'b', follow these steps closely and in sequence:

1. **Feast object 'c' from object 'b'**: 
   - To initiate this action, ensure that object 'c' craves object 'b', object 'c' is a province, and harmony is present.
   - As a result, object 'c' will be in pain, object 'b' will become a province, while object 'c' will no longer crave 'b', will no longer be a province, and harmony will cease.

2. **Succumb object 'c'**:
   - For object 'c' to succumb, it must be in pain.
   - Completing this action restores object 'c' to a province, restores object 'b' to a planet, and harmony is regained. Object 'c' will no longer be in pain.

3. **Feast object 'd' from object 'a'**:
   - Ensure object 'd' craves object 'a', object 'd' is a province, and harmony exists before starting.
   - The effect of this step will cause object 'd' to be in pain, object 'a' will become a province, and object 'd' will no longer crave 'a', be a province, or have harmony.

4. **Succumb object 'd'**:
   - Object 'd' can succumb only if it is in pain.
   - As a result, object 'd' will return to being a province, object 'a' will be a planet again, and harmony is restored. Object 'd' will also no longer be in pain.

5. **Attack object 'a'**:
   - Action preconditions require that object 'a' is a province and a planet, and harmony is present.
   - This action results in object 'a' experiencing pain, and it will no longer be a province or planet. Harmony will be lost.

6. **Overcome object 'a' from object 'b'**:
   - This action can begin if object 'b' is a province and object 'a' is experiencing pain.
   - After this step, harmony is restored; object 'a' becomes a province, craves object 'b', and object 'b' will no longer be a province. Object 'a' will also no longer be in pain.

By following these steps, the desired goal state is achieved, where object 'a' craves object 'b'.

    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.

    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.

    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

FINAL-PLAN

```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain object_state_transitions)

  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x ?y - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem instance_1)

  (:domain object_state_transitions)

  (:objects
    a b c d - object
  )

  (:init
    (craves a b)
    (craves b c)
    (craves c d)
    (harmony)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
  )

  (:goal
    (and 
      (craves b c)
      (craves c d)
      (craves d a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is a human-readable version of the PDDL plan that carefully follows each action step-by-step:

1. **Feast Operation on Objects A and B**  
   Object A is in a harmonious state and craves object B. This craving allows A to feast on B, sacrificing its province status and disrupting harmony, resulting in object A experiencing pain.

2. **Succumb to Pain by Object A**  
   The pain experienced by object A is resolved as it reattains its status as a province and a planet, restoring harmony to itself.

3. **Feast Operation on Objects B and C**  
   Now, object B is in a harmonious state and craves object C. This craving allows B to feast on C, causing object B to experience pain, losing its province status, and again disrupting harmony.

4. **Succumb to Pain by Object B**  
   The pain experienced by object B is alleviated as it regains its province and planet characteristics, reintegrating harmony.

5. **Feast Operation on Objects C and D**  
   Object C craves object D, with a harmonious state enabling it to feast on D. This action results in object C gaining pain, losing its province status, and causing a break in harmony.

6. **Succumb to Pain by Object C**  
   The pain on object C is mitigated as it regains the attributes of a province and planet, restoring harmony.

7. **Attack Operation on Object D**  
   Object D, located on a planet and under harmonious conditions, experiences an attack action, leading to D experiencing pain, losing its province and planet attributes, and disrupting harmony again.

8. **Overcome by Object D onto Object A**  
   Object D, still in pain and a separate province entity, overcomes the condition by engaging with object A, allowing harmony to be reestablished with object D becoming the province that craves object A. Object D's pain is eliminated.

9. **Attack Operation on Object C**  
   Similar to previous attacks, object C undergoes an attack, causing pain and disruption to its province and harmonious conditions.

10. **Overcome by Object C onto Object D**  
   Object C, affected by pain, overcomes this condition by moving towards object D, reestablishing harmony and converting into a province that craves object D. The pain is thus removed.

11. **Attack Operation on Object B**  
   Object B is attacked, leading to a similar sequence where pain prevails, disrupting all holdings of province characteristics and existing harmony.

12. **Overcome by Object B onto Object C**  
   Finally, object B overcomes its painful condition by aligning again with object C. This transition makes object B crave object C, aligning with its goal, and restoring all prior attributes including harmonious conditions, thereby completing the sequence.

Overall, this sequence of actions successfully achieves the initial goal, with each step respecting the constraints and transformations defined by the domain.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
```pddl
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types object)
  (:predicates 
    (planet ?x - object)
    (province ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )
  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (not (province ?x)) (not (planet ?x)) (not (harmony)) (pain ?x))
  )
  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (pain ?x)
    :effect (and (province ?x) (planet ?x) (harmony) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (province ?y) (pain ?x))
    :effect (and (harmony) (province ?x) (craves ?x ?y) (not (province ?y)) (not (pain ?x)))
  )
  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and (craves ?x ?y) (province ?x) (harmony))
    :effect (and (pain ?x) (province ?y) (not (craves ?x ?y)) (not (province ?x)) (not (harmony)))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects 
    a b c d - object
  )
  (:init 
    (craves b d)
    (craves c b)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
    (province c)
  )
  (:goal (and
    (craves a c)
    (craves b a)
  ))
)
</problem>
```

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having object 'a' crave object 'c' and object 'b' crave object 'a', you will need to follow the sequence of actions outlined below, based on the provided PDDL plan. Each step is a direct translation of the actions specified and will guide you through the process sequentially:

1. **Feast (c, b)**
   - Action: Object 'c' feasts on object 'b' while 'c' is in its province and harmony exists.
   - Effect: 'c' experiences pain, object 'b' becomes part of the province, 'c' no longer craves 'b', and harmony is disrupted.

2. **Succumb (c)**
   - Action: Object 'c', currently in pain, succumbs.
   - Effect: 'c' returns to being a province and a planet, and harmony is restored. The pain of 'c' is alleviated.

3. **Attack (a)**
   - Action: Object 'a', existing as a province and a planet while harmony exists, attacks.
   - Effect: 'a' experiences pain, ceases to be a province or planet, and harmony is disrupted.

4. **Overcome (a, c)**
   - Action: Object 'a', which is experiencing pain, overcomes object 'c', which is a province.
   - Effect: Harmony is restored, 'a' becomes a province and craves 'c', 'c' ceases to be a province, and the pain of 'a' is alleviated.

5. **Feast (b, d)**
   - Action: Object 'b', currently in the province and craving 'd', feasts on 'd' while harmony exists.
   - Effect: 'b' experiences pain, object 'd' becomes part of the province, 'b' no longer craves 'd', and harmony is disrupted.

6. **Overcome (b, a)**
   - Action: Object 'b', which is in pain, overcomes object 'a' which is now a province.
   - Effect: Harmony is restored, 'b' becomes a province and craves 'a', 'a' ceases to be a province, and the pain of 'b' is alleviated.

By following these actions, you should reach the desired final state where 'a' craves 'c' and 'b' craves 'a', satisfying the goal in the plan.
